Ki ar ngut ki rangbah synshar ki la bitar bad ki la die ïa u Joseph sha Ijipt, hynrei U Blei u la don lem bad u.
Ka jingtip jong u ka pynlong ïa ki duriaw-bah ban pyut, bad ki lyoh slap ban hap noh.
Kumta shisyndon la don lai ngut ki rangbah kiba la wan phai na Kaisaria sha ka ïing jong nga.
Hynrei ïa ki ïing jong ki nongrit ki bym don kynroh sawdong jong ki yn sa ñiew ba ki long kum ki lyngkha ha ka rudnong. Ïa ki lah ban pyllaitluid bad ban mihnoh ha ka Snem ka Jingpynphai pat.
U Trai U Blei Badonbor Baroh u ong, Ka jingsniew jong me ka long kaba sniew, bad ka jinglong bymïaineh jong me ka long kaba kthang. Me la iehnoh ïa nga, U Trai U Blei jong me, bad me tip ba ngam shym don ha ka jingtiengburom ïa me.
U shna ka durkhmat kaba da ki tyllai kiba rit, bad nangta u beh noh ïa ki briew baroh na ka Templ, kynthup ïa ki langbrot bad ki masi, bad kynthup ruh ïa ki sbai rupa. U theh noh ïa ka pisa na ki nongkhaïi bad pynkylla khongpong ïa ki miej jong ki nongïalam.
